				<div id="bodyContent" class="vector-body" aria-labelledby="firstHeading" data-mw-ve-target-container>

		</div>

						<div id="siteSub" class="noprint">From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia</div>
						<div id="contentSub"><div id="mw-content-subtitle"></div></div>
						
					</div>
					
					<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content"><div class="mw-content-ltr mw-parser-output" lang="en" dir="ltr" data-mw-parsoid-version="0.24.0.0-alpha27" data-mw-html-version="2.8.0"><section data-mw-section-id="0" id="mwAQ"><div class="shortdescription nomobile noexcerpt noprint searchaux" style="display:none" about="#mwt1" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Short description","href":"./Template:Short_description"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Non-parametric statistic used to estimate the survival function"}},"i":0}}]}'>Non-parametric statistic used to estimate the survival function</div><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt1"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_short_description"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Short_description_matches_Wikidata"/></span>
<p class="mw-empty-elt" id="mwAw"><span typeof="mw:Nowiki mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt2" id="mwBA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Use mdy dates","href":"./Template:Use_mdy_dates"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"August 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Use_mdy_dates_from_August_2022" about="#mwt2" id="mwBQ"/></p>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb" id="mwBg"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Km_plot.jpg" class="mw-file-description" id="mwBw"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Km_plot.jpg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/73/Km_plot.jpg/250px-Km_plot.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/7/73/Km_plot.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail_unscaled 2x" data-file-width="384" data-file-height="331" data-file-type="bitmap" height="215" width="250" class="mw-file-element" id="mwCA"/></a><figcaption id="mwCQ">An example of a Kaplan–Meier plot for two conditions associated with patient survival.</figcaption></figure>

<p id="mwCg">The <b id="mwCw">Kaplan–Meier estimator</b>,<sup about="#mwt5" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-1" id="mwDA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwDQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwDg">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwDw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt7" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2" id="mwEA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwEQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEg">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEw">]</span></span></a></sup> also known as the <b id="mwFA">product limit estimator</b>, is a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-parametric_statistics" title="Non-parametric statistics" class="mw-redirect" id="mwFQ">non-parametric</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Statistic" title="Statistic" id="mwFg">statistic</a> used to estimate the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Survival_function" title="Survival function" id="mwFw">survival function</a> from lifetime data.  In medical research, it is often used to measure the fraction of patients living for a certain amount of time after treatment. In other fields, Kaplan–Meier estimators may be used to measure the length of time people remain unemployed after a job loss,<sup about="#mwt10" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-3" id="mwGA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwGQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGg">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGw">]</span></span></a></sup> the time-to-failure of machine parts, or how long fleshy fruits remain on plants before they are removed by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frugivore" title="Frugivore" id="mwHA">frugivores</a>. The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Estimator" title="Estimator" id="mwHQ">estimator</a> is named after <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edward_L._Kaplan" title="Edward L. Kaplan" id="mwHg">Edward L. Kaplan</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Meier_(statistician)" title="Paul Meier (statistician)" id="mwHw">Paul Meier</a>, who each submitted similar manuscripts to the <i id="mwIA"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Journal_of_the_American_Statistical_Association" title="Journal of the American Statistical Association" id="mwIQ">Journal of the American Statistical Association</a></i>.<sup about="#mwt13" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-4" id="mwIg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJA">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJQ">]</span></span></a></sup> The journal editor, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Tukey" title="John Tukey" id="mwJg">John Tukey</a>, convinced them to combine their work into one paper, which has been cited more than 34,000 times since its publication in 1958.<sup about="#mwt16" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-5" id="mwJw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwKA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKQ">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt19" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwKw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwLA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLQ">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwLg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwLw">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Estimator" title="Estimator" id="mwMA">estimator</a> of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Survival_function" title="Survival function" id="mwMQ">survival function</a> <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt20" id="mwMg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S(t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

<p id="mwNw">with <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt23" id="mwOA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"t_i"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle t_{i}}">

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwQQ" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwQg" aria-labelledby="Basic_concepts"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Basic_concepts">Basic concepts</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Basic concepts"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwQw">A plot of the Kaplan–Meier estimator is a series of declining horizontal steps which, with a large enough sample size, approaches the true survival function for that population. The value of the survival function between successive distinct sampled observations ("clicks") is assumed to be constant.</p>

<p id="mwRA">An important advantage of the Kaplan–Meier curve is that the method can take into account some types of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Censoring_(statistics)" title="Censoring (statistics)" id="mwRQ">censored data</a>, particularly <i id="mwRg">right-censoring</i>, which occurs if a patient withdraws from a study, is <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lost_to_follow-up" title="Lost to follow-up" id="mwRw">lost to follow-up</a>, or is alive without event occurrence at last follow-up.  On the plot, small vertical tick-marks state individual patients whose survival times have been right-censored.  When no truncation or censoring occurs, the Kaplan–Meier curve is the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Complementary_event" title="Complementary event" id="mwSA">complement</a> of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Empirical_distribution_function" title="Empirical distribution function" id="mwSQ">empirical distribution function</a>.</p>

<p id="mwSg">In <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medical_statistics" title="Medical statistics" id="mwSw">medical statistics</a>, a typical application might involve grouping patients into categories, for instance, those with Gene A profile and those with Gene B profile.  In the graph, patients with Gene B die much quicker than those with Gene A. After two years, about 80% of the Gene A patients survive, but less than half of patients with Gene B.</p>

<p id="mwTA">To generate a Kaplan–Meier estimator, at least two pieces of data are required for each patient (or each subject): the status at last observation (event occurrence or right-censored), and the time to event (or time to censoring). If the survival functions between two or more groups are to be compared, then a third piece of data is required: the group assignment of each subject.<sup about="#mwt29" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-km-explain_7-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"km-explain"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-km-explain-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-km-explain-7" id="mwTQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwTg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTw">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwUQ" aria-labelledby="Problem_definition"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Problem_definition">Problem definition</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Problem definition"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwUg">Let <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt30" id="mwUw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\tau\\ge 0"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \tau \geq 0}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>τ<!-- τ --></mi>
        <mo>≥<!-- ≥ --></mo>
        <mn>0</mn>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle \tau \geq 0}</annotation>
  </semantics>

  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/c680d6458093d7fdf32ae81c75f39ed936df9a5d" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -1.005ex; width:15.937ex; height:3.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\tilde {\tau }}_{j}=\min(\tau _{j},c_{j})}"/></span>. In particular, the information available about the timing of event <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt48" id="mwag" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"j"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle j}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>j</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle j}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/2f461e54f5c093e92a55547b9764291390f0b5d0" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; margin-left: -0.027ex; width:0.985ex; height:2.509ex;" alt="{\displaystyle j}"/></span> is whether the event happened before the fixed time <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt49" id="mwaw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"c_j"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle c_{j}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>c</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>j</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle c_{j}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/a844d180d176af828d1636d4e85aa534d0b77baa" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -1.005ex; width:1.917ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle c_{j}}"/></span> and if so, then the actual time of the event is also available. The challenge is to estimate <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt50" id="mwbA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S(t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>S</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S(t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ed5c98226fcae6540afa928ccb8c2245844ac0a8" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.148ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S(t)}"/></span> given this data.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwbQ" aria-labelledby="Derivation_of_the_Kaplan–Meier_estimator"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Derivation_of_the_Kaplan–Meier_estimator"><span id="Derivation_of_the_Kaplan.E2.80.93Meier_estimator" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>Derivation of the Kaplan–Meier estimator</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Derivation of the Kaplan–Meier estimator"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwbg">Two derivations of the Kaplan–Meier estimator are shown. Both are based on rewriting the survival function in terms of what is sometimes called <b id="mwbw">hazard</b>, or <b id="mwcA">mortality rates</b>. However, before doing this it is worthwhile to consider a naive estimator.</p>

<section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwcQ" aria-labelledby="A_naive_estimator"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="A_naive_estimator">A naive estimator</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: A naive estimator"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwcg">To understand the power of the Kaplan–Meier estimator, it is worthwhile to first describe a naive estimator of the survival function.</p>

<p id="mwcw">Fix <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt51" id="mwdA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k\\in [n]:=\\{1,\\dots,n\\}"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k\in [n]:=\{1,\dots ,n\}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">

        <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>

<p id="mwlQ">where the second equality follows because <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt74" id="mwlg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" \\tilde \\tau_k\\ge t  "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\tilde {\tau }}_{k}\geq t}">

</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/1b74842dd65600741be1f3d5aed55700fe12e15f" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:6.394ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\tilde {\tau }}_{k}\geq t}"/></span> implies <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt75" id="mwlw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" c_k\\ge t "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle c_{k}\geq t}">
  <semantics>

<p id="mwmA">The quality of this estimate is governed by the size of <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt76" id="mwmQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"m(t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle m(t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle m(t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ea26578dd72bf4dbc3fa391c9feb11eed495699b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.689ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle m(t)}"/></span>. This can be problematic when <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt77" id="mwmg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"m(t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle m(t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>m</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle m(t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ea26578dd72bf4dbc3fa391c9feb11eed495699b" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.689ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle m(t)}"/></span> is small, which happens, by definition, when a lot of the events are censored. A particularly unpleasant property of this estimator, that suggests that perhaps it is not the "best" estimator, is that it ignores all the observations whose censoring time precedes <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt78" id="mwmw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"t"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle t}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>t</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle t}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/65658b7b223af9e1acc877d848888ecdb4466560" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.338ex; width:0.84ex; height:2.009ex;" alt="{\displaystyle t}"/></span>. Intuitively, these observations still contain information about <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt79" id="mwnA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S(t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>S</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S(t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ed5c98226fcae6540afa928ccb8c2245844ac0a8" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.148ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S(t)}"/></span>: For example, when for many events with <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt80" id="mwnQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"c_k &lt; t "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle c_{k}&lt;t}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>c</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
        <mo>&lt;</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle c_{k}&lt;t}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/83e2299836d79132720dca4924eac1b604c113fa" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:6.034ex; height:2.343ex;" alt="{\displaystyle c_{k}&lt;t}"/></span>, <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt81" id="mwng" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\tau_k&lt;c_k "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \tau _{k}&lt;c_{k}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>τ<!-- τ --></mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>

        <msub>
          <mi>c</mi>

      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle \tau _{k}&lt;c_{k}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/18f4892f26edc001cc40de7144c9b001165d2660" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.671ex; width:7.299ex; height:2.176ex;" alt="{\displaystyle \tau _{k}&lt;c_{k}}"/></span> also holds, we can infer that events often happen early, which implies that <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt82" id="mwnw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"\\operatorname{Prob}(\\tau\\le t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \operatorname {Prob} (\tau \leq t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>Prob</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>τ<!-- τ --></mi>

        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>

    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle \operatorname {Prob} (\tau \leq t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/9bd8848ffd87fc484cadb9efbd75a63b89950ca5" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:11.899ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle \operatorname {Prob} (\tau \leq t)}"/></span> is large, which, through <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt83" id="mwoA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S(t) = 1-\\operatorname{Prob}(\\tau\\le t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau \leq t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>S</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
        <mi>Prob</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>

        <mi>t</mi>

      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S(t)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau \leq t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/db4dcfc723bfc94d62da2bb6ba0922af603395e1" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:23.148ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S(t)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau \leq t)}"/></span> means that <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt84" id="mwoQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S(t)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>S</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S(t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ed5c98226fcae6540afa928ccb8c2245844ac0a8" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.148ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S(t)}"/></span> must be small. However, this information is ignored by this naive estimator. The question is then whether there exists an estimator that makes a better use of all the data. This is what the Kaplan–Meier estimator accomplishes. Note that the naive estimator cannot be improved when censoring does not take place; so whether an improvement is possible critically hinges upon whether censoring is in place.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwog" aria-labelledby="The_plug-in_approach"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="The_plug-in_approach">The plug-in approach</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: The plug-in approach"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwow">By elementary calculations,</p>

<p id="mwrA">By a recursive expansion of the equality <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt88" id="mwrQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"S(t) = q(t) S(t-1)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)=q(t)S(t-1)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

        <mi>S</mi>

        <mo>=</mo>

        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>

        <mi>t</mi>

        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S(t)=q(t)S(t-1)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/67dd44c5b7fa7fba0f0831de5b88c857a27afbfe" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:19.116ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S(t)=q(t)S(t-1)}"/></span>, we get</p>

<dl id="mwrg"><dd id="mwrw"><span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt89" id="mwsA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" S(t) = q(t) q(t-1) \\cdots q(0). "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)=q(t)q(t-1)\cdots q(0).}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>S</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mi>q</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mi>q</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>⋯<!-- ⋯ --></mo>
        <mi>q</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mn>0</mn>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>.</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S(t)=q(t)q(t-1)\cdots q(0).}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/9c6fd98295d0b1d160b0dee942b967ed119ecead" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:26.872ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S(t)=q(t)q(t-1)\cdots q(0).}"/></span></dd></dl>

<p id="mwsQ">Note that here <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt90" id="mwsg" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"q(0) = 1-\\operatorname{Prob}(\\tau=0\\mid\\tau > -1) = 1-\\operatorname{Prob}(\\tau=0)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle q(0)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau =0\mid \tau >-1)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau =0)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>q</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mn>0</mn>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
        <mi>Prob</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>τ<!-- τ --></mi>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mn>0</mn>

        <mi>τ<!-- τ --></mi>
        <mo>></mo>
        <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
        <mo>=</mo>
        <mn>1</mn>
        <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
        <mi>Prob</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>

    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle q(0)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau =0\mid \tau >-1)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau =0)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ee6748b148a77a645d796ec51b85c622d0e84559" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:51.895ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle q(0)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau =0\mid \tau >-1)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau =0)}"/></span>.</p>

<p id="mwsw">The Kaplan–Meier estimator can be seen as a "plug-in estimator" where each <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt91" id="mwtA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" q(s)\n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle q(s)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>q</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>s</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle q(s)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/39aea2a3cf9a60a8596f05335ba3e293cf545e7a" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:3.969ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle q(s)}"/></span> is estimated based on the data and the estimator of <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt92" id="mwtQ" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" S(t)\n"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle S(t)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>S</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
        <mi>t</mi>
        <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle S(t)}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ed5c98226fcae6540afa928ccb8c2245844ac0a8" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -0.838ex; width:4.148ex; height:2.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle S(t)}"/></span> is obtained as a product of these estimates.</p>

<p id="mwtg">It remains to specify how <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt93" id="mwtw" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"q(s)=1-\\operatorname{Prob}(\\tau=s\\mid\\tau\\ge s)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle q(s)=1-\operatorname {Prob} (\tau =s\mid \tau \geq s)}">

By a similar reasoning that lead to the construction of the naive estimator above, for any <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt94" id="mwuA" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"k\\in [n]"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle k\in [n]}">

<p id="mwwQ">Hence we arrive at the estimator</p>

    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\hat {q}}(s)=1-{\frac {|\{1\leq k\leq n\,:\,c_{k}>s,{\tilde {\tau }}_{k}=s\}|}{|\{1\leq k\leq n\,:\,c_{k}>s,{\tilde {\tau }}_{k}\geq s\}|}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>

<p id="mw3Q">As opposed to the naive estimator, this estimator can be seen to use the available information more effectively: In the special case mentioned beforehand, when there are many early events recorded, the estimator will multiply many terms with a value below one and will thus take into account that the survival probability cannot be large.</p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mw3g" aria-labelledby="Derivation_as_a_maximum_likelihood_estimator"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Derivation_as_a_maximum_likelihood_estimator">Derivation as a maximum likelihood estimator</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Derivation as a maximum likelihood estimator"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mw3w">Kaplan–Meier estimator can be derived from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maximum_likelihood_estimation" title="Maximum likelihood estimation" id="mw4A">maximum likelihood estimation</a> of the discrete <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hazard_function" title="Hazard function" class="mw-redirect" id="mw4Q">hazard function</a>.<sup about="#mwt121" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-8" id="mw4g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw4w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5A">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5Q">]</span></span></a></sup> More specifically given <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt122" id="mw5g" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":"d_i"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle d_{i}}">

<p id="mw9Q">therefore the log likelihood will be:</p>

<dl id="mw9g"><dd id="mw9w"><span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt130" id="mw-A" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" \\log(\\mathcal{L}) = \\sum_{j=1}^i \\left(d_j\\log(h_j)+(n_j-d_j)\\log(1-h_j) + \\log {n_j \\choose d_j} \\right)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \log({\mathcal {L}})=\sum _{j=1}^{i}\left(d_{j}\log(h_{j})+(n_{j}-d_{j})\log(1-h_{j})+\log {n_{j} \choose d_{j}}\right)}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mi>log</mi>
        <mo>⁡<!-- ⁡ --></mo>

<p id="mwAQI">More generally (for continuous as well as discrete survival distributions), the Kaplan-Meier estimator may be interpreted as a nonparametric maximum likelihood estimator.<sup about="#mwt136" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwAQM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQU">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQY">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwAQc" aria-labelledby="Benefits_and_limitations"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Benefits_and_limitations">Benefits and limitations</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Benefits and limitations"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAQg">The Kaplan–Meier estimator is one of the most frequently used methods of survival analysis. The estimate may be useful to examine recovery rates, the probability of death, and the effectiveness of treatment. It is limited in its ability to estimate survival adjusted for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Covariate" title="Covariate" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAQk">covariates</a>; parametric <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Survival_function" title="Survival function" id="mwAQo">survival models</a> and the Cox <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proportional_hazards_model" title="Proportional hazards model" id="mwAQs">proportional hazards model</a> may be useful to estimate covariate-adjusted survival.</p>

<p id="mwAQw">The Kaplan-Meier estimator is directly related to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nelson-Aalen_estimator" title="Nelson-Aalen estimator" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAQ0">Nelson-Aalen estimator</a> and both maximize the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Empirical_likelihood" title="Empirical likelihood" id="mwAQ4">empirical likelihood</a>.<sup about="#mwt137" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwAQ8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARE">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARI">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwARM" aria-labelledby="Statistical_considerations"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Statistical_considerations">Statistical considerations</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Statistical considerations"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwARQ">The Kaplan–Meier estimator is a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Statistic" title="Statistic" id="mwARU">statistic</a>, and several estimators are used to approximate its <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Variance" title="Variance" id="mwARY">variance</a>.  One of the most common estimators is Greenwood's formula:<sup about="#mwt140" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-11" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-11" id="mwARc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARk">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARo">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<dl id="mwARs"><dd id="mwARw"><span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt141" id="mwAR0" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" \\widehat{\\operatorname{Var}} \\left( \\widehat S(t) \\right) = \\widehat S(t)^2  \\sum_{i:\\ t_i\\le t} \\frac{d_i}{n_i(n_i-d_i)},"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle {\widehat {\operatorname {Var} }}\left({\widehat {S}}(t)\right)={\widehat {S}}(t)^{2}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}{\frac {d_{i}}{n_{i}(n_{i}-d_{i})}},}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

            <mtext> </mtext>
            <msub>
              <mi>t</mi>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

              </mrow>
            </msub>
            <mo>≤<!-- ≤ --></mo>
            <mi>t</mi>
          </mrow>
        </munder>
        <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">

            <msub>
              <mi>d</mi>
              <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                <mi>i</mi>
              </mrow>

            <mrow>

                <mi>n</mi>
                <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                  <mi>i</mi>
                </mrow>

              <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
              <msub>
                <mi>n</mi>
                <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                  <mi>i</mi>
                </mrow>
              </msub>
              <mo>−<!-- − --></mo>
              <msub>
                <mi>d</mi>
                <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
                  <mi>i</mi>

    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\widehat {\operatorname {Var} }}\left({\widehat {S}}(t)\right)={\widehat {S}}(t)^{2}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}{\frac {d_{i}}{n_{i}(n_{i}-d_{i})}},}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/e040cc23bbd223b79fcb7b2ae060a859f429297e" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -3.338ex; width:37.706ex; height:6.843ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\widehat {\operatorname {Var} }}\left({\widehat {S}}(t)\right)={\widehat {S}}(t)^{2}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}{\frac {d_{i}}{n_{i}(n_{i}-d_{i})}},}"/></span></dd></dl>

<p id="mwAR4">where <span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt142" id="mwAR8" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" d_i "}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle d_{i}}">
  <semantics>
    <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
      <mstyle displaystyle="true" scriptlevel="0">
        <msub>
          <mi>d</mi>
          <mrow class="MJX-TeXAtom-ORD">
            <mi>i</mi>
          </mrow>
        </msub>
      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle d_{i}}</annotation>

<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt145" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwASI" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Hidden","href":"./Template:Hidden"},"params":{"titlestyle":{"wt":"background-color:#f2dfce; color:black;"},"contentstyle":{"wt":"border:1px #C4C3D0 solid;"},"header":{"wt":"For a &apos;sketch&apos; of the mathematical derivation of the equation above, click on \"show\" to reveal"},"content":{"wt":"Greenwood&apos;s formula is derived&lt;ref name=\":0\">{{Cite web|url=https://www.math.wustl.edu/%7Esawyer/handouts/greenwood.pdf|title=The Greenwood and Exponential Greenwood Confidence Intervals in Survival Analysis|accessdate=12 May 2023}}&lt;/ref>{{self-published inline|date=November 2021}} by noting that probability of getting &lt;math>d_i&lt;/math> failures out of &lt;math> n_i&lt;/math> cases follows a [[binomial distribution]] with failure probability &lt;math>h_i&lt;/math>. As a result for maximum likelihood hazard rate &lt;math>\\widehat{h}_i = d_i/n_i&lt;/math> we have &lt;math>E\\left(\\widehat{h}_i\\right) = h_i\n&lt;/math> and &lt;math>\\operatorname{Var}\\left(\\widehat{h}_i\\right) = h_i(1-h_i)/n_i&lt;/math>. To avoid dealing with multiplicative probabilities we compute variance of logarithm of &lt;math> \\widehat S(t)&lt;/math> and will use the [[delta method]] to convert it back to the original variance:\n\n: &lt;math>\\begin{align}\n\\operatorname{Var}\\left(\\log \\widehat{S}(t)\\right) &amp;\\sim \\frac{1}{{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2} \\operatorname{Var} \\left(\\widehat{S}(t)\\right) \\Rightarrow \\\\\n\\operatorname{Var}\\left( \\widehat{S}(t)\\right) &amp;\\sim {{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2}\\operatorname{Var}\\left(\\log\\widehat{S}(t) \\right)\n\\end{align}&lt;/math>\n\nusing [[martingale central limit theorem]], it can be shown that the variance of the sum in the following equation is equal to the sum of variances:&lt;ref name=\":0\" />\n\n: &lt;math> \\log\\widehat S(t) = \\sum\\limits_{i:\\ t_i\\le t} \\log\\left(1 - \\widehat{h}_i\\right)&lt;/math>\n\nas a result we can write:\n\n: &lt;math>\\begin{align}\n\\operatorname{Var}( \\widehat{S}(t)) &amp;\\sim \n{{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2}\\operatorname{Var}\\left(\\sum_{i:\\ t_i\\le t} \\log\\left(1 - \\widehat{h}_i\\right)\\right) \\\\\n&amp;\\sim\n{{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2}\\sum\\limits_{i:\\ t_i\\le t} \\operatorname{Var}\\left(\\log\\left(1 - \\widehat{h}_i\\right)\\right)\n\\end{align}&lt;/math>\n\nusing the delta method once more:\n\n: &lt;math>\\begin{align}\n\\operatorname{Var}( \\widehat{S}(t)) &amp;\\sim \n{{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2}\\sum_{i:\\ t_i\\le t}\\left(\\frac{\\partial \\log\\left(1 - \\widehat{h}_i\\right)}{\\partial \\widehat{h}_i}\\right)^2 \\operatorname{Var}\\left(\\widehat{h}_i\\right)\\\\\n&amp;={{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2}\\sum_{i:\\ t_i\\le t}\\left(\\frac{1}{1-\\widehat{h}_i}\\right)^2\\frac{\\widehat{h}_i \\left( 1-\\widehat{h}_i \\right)}{n_i} \\\\\n&amp;= {{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2}\\sum_{i:\\ t_i\\le t} \\frac{\\widehat{h}_i}{n_i\\left(1-\\widehat{h}_i\\right)} \\\\\n&amp;= {{\\widehat{S}(t)}^2}\\sum_{i:\\ t_i\\le t} \\frac{d_i}{n_i(n_i-d_i)}\n\\end{align}&lt;/math>\n\nas desired.\n----"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1214851843" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt146" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Hidden begin/styles.css"}}'>.mw-parser-output .hidden-begin{box-sizing:border-box;width:100%;padding:5px;border:none;font-size:95%}.mw-parser-output .hidden-title{font-weight:bold;line-height:1.6;text-align:left}.mw-parser-output .hidden-content{text-align:left}@media all and (max-width:500px){.mw-parser-output .hidden-begin{width:auto!important;clear:none!important;float:none!important}}</style></span><div class="hidden-begin mw-collapsible  mw-collapsed " style="   " about="#mwt145" id="mwASM"><div class="hidden-title skin-nightmode-reset-color" style="text-align:center;  background-color:#f2dfce; color:black;">For a 'sketch' of the mathematical derivation of the equation above, click on "show" to reveal</div><div class="hidden-content mw-collapsible-content" style="  border:1px #C4C3D0 solid;">

    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\begin{aligned}\operatorname {Var} \left(\log {\widehat {S}}(t)\right)&amp;\sim {\frac {1}{{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}}\operatorname {Var} \left({\widehat {S}}(t)\right)\Rightarrow \\\operatorname {Var} \left({\widehat {S}}(t)\right)&amp;\sim {{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\operatorname {Var} \left(\log {\widehat {S}}(t)\right)\end{aligned}}}</annotation>

<p>using <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Martingale_central_limit_theorem" title="Martingale central limit theorem">martingale central limit theorem</a>, it can be shown that the variance of the sum in the following equation is equal to the sum of variances:<sup about="#mwt160" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_12-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-12" id="mwASg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASo">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASs">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<dl><dd><span class="mwe-math-element mwe-math-element-inline" typeof="mw:Extension/math" about="#mwt162" data-mw='{"name":"math","attrs":{},"body":{"extsrc":" \\log\\widehat S(t) = \\sum\\limits_{i:\\ t_i\\le t} \\log\\left(1 - \\widehat{h}_i\\right)"}}'><span class="mwe-math-mathml-a11y" style="display: none;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" class="mathjax_ignore" alttext="{\displaystyle \log {\widehat {S}}(t)=\sum \limits _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}\log \left(1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)}">

      </mstyle>
    </mrow>
    <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">{\displaystyle {\begin{aligned}\operatorname {Var} ({\widehat {S}}(t))&amp;\sim {{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}\left({\frac {\partial \log \left(1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)}{\partial {\widehat {h}}_{i}}}\right)^{2}\operatorname {Var} \left({\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)\\&amp;={{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}\left({\frac {1}{1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}}}\right)^{2}{\frac {{\widehat {h}}_{i}\left(1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)}{n_{i}}}\\&amp;={{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}{\frac {{\widehat {h}}_{i}}{n_{i}\left(1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)}}\\&amp;={{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}{\frac {d_{i}}{n_{i}(n_{i}-d_{i})}}\end{aligned}}}</annotation>
  </semantics>
</math></span><img src="https://wikimedia.org/api/rest_v1/media/math/render/svg/ec84cee654e1c95e9d19562df4c82a6b93616100" class="mwe-math-fallback-image-inline mw-invert skin-invert" aria-hidden="true" style="vertical-align: -16.94ex; margin-bottom: -0.232ex; width:53.244ex; height:35.509ex;" alt="{\displaystyle {\begin{aligned}\operatorname {Var} ({\widehat {S}}(t))&amp;\sim {{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}\left({\frac {\partial \log \left(1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)}{\partial {\widehat {h}}_{i}}}\right)^{2}\operatorname {Var} \left({\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)\\&amp;={{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}\left({\frac {1}{1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}}}\right)^{2}{\frac {{\widehat {h}}_{i}\left(1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)}{n_{i}}}\\&amp;={{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}{\frac {{\widehat {h}}_{i}}{n_{i}\left(1-{\widehat {h}}_{i}\right)}}\\&amp;={{{\widehat {S}}(t)}^{2}}\sum _{i:\ t_{i}\leq t}{\frac {d_{i}}{n_{i}(n_{i}-d_{i})}}\end{aligned}}}"/></span></dd></dl>

<p>as desired.</p>
<hr/></div></div>
<p id="mwASw">In some cases, one may wish to compare different Kaplan–Meier curves. This can be done by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Log_rank_test" title="Log rank test" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAS0">log rank test</a>, and the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proportional_hazards_models" title="Proportional hazards models" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAS4">Cox proportional hazards test</a>.</p>

<p id="mwAS8">Other statistics that may be of use with this estimator are pointwise confidence intervals,<sup about="#mwt167" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-13" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-13" id="mwATA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATI">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATM">]</span></span></a></sup> the Hall-Wellner band<sup about="#mwt170" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Hall1980_14-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Hall1980"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Hall1980-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Hall1980-14" id="mwATQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATY">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATc">]</span></span></a></sup> and the equal-precision band.<sup about="#mwt173" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Nair1984_15-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Nair1984"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Nair1984-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Nair1984-15" id="mwATg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATo">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATs">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwATw" aria-labelledby="Software"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Software">Software</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Kaplan%E2%80%93Meier_estimator&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Software"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul id="mwAT0"><li id="mwAT4"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mathematica" title="Mathematica" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAT8">Mathematica</a>: the built-in function <code id="mwAUA">SurvivalModelFit</code> creates survival models.<sup about="#mwt176" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-16" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-16" id="mwAUE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUM">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUQ">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAUU"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SAS_(software)" title="SAS (software)" id="mwAUY">SAS</a>: The Kaplan–Meier estimator is implemented in the <code id="mwAUc">proc lifetest</code> procedure.<sup about="#mwt179" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwAUg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUo">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUs">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAUw"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/R_(programming_language)" title="R (programming language)" id="mwAU0">R</a>: the Kaplan–Meier estimator is available as part of the <code id="mwAU4">survival</code> package.<sup about="#mwt182" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-18" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-18" id="mwAU8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVE">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVI">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt185" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-19" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-19"}}'><a href="#cite_note-19" id="mwAVM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVU">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVY">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt188" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-20" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-20"}}'><a href="#cite_note-20" id="mwAVc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVk">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVo">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAVs"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stata" title="Stata" id="mwAVw">Stata</a>: the command <code id="mwAV0">sts</code> returns the Kaplan–Meier estimator.<sup about="#mwt191" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-21" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-21"}}'><a href="#cite_note-21" id="mwAV4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAV8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWA">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt194" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-22" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-22"}}'><a href="#cite_note-22" id="mwAWI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAWM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWQ">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWU">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAWY"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Python_(programming_language)" title="Python (programming language)" id="mwAWc">Python</a>: the <code id="mwAWg">lifelines</code> and <code id="mwAWk">scikit-survival</code> packages each include the Kaplan–Meier estimator.<sup about="#mwt197" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-23" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-23"}}'><a href="#cite_note-23" id="mwAWo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAWs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWw">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAW0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt200" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-24" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-24"}}'><a href="#cite_note-24" id="mwAW4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAW8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXA">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXE">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAXI"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MATLAB" title="MATLAB" id="mwAXM">MATLAB</a>: the <code id="mwAXQ">ecdf</code> function with the <code id="mwAXU">'function','survivor'</code> arguments can calculate or plot the Kaplan–Meier estimator.<sup about="#mwt203" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-25" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-25"}}'><a href="#cite_note-25" id="mwAXY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXg">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXk">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAXo"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/StatsDirect" title="StatsDirect" id="mwAXs">StatsDirect</a>: The Kaplan–Meier estimator is implemented in the <code id="mwAXw">Survival Analysis</code> menu.<sup about="#mwt206" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-26" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-26"}}'><a href="#cite_note-26" id="mwAX0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAX4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAX8">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYA">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAYE"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SPSS" title="SPSS" id="mwAYI">SPSS</a>: The Kaplan–Meier estimator is implemented in the <code id="mwAYM">Analyze > Survival > Kaplan-Meier...</code> menu.<sup about="#mwt209" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-27" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-27"}}'><a href="#cite_note-27" id="mwAYQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAYU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYY">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYc">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAYg"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Julia_(programming_language)" title="Julia (programming language)" id="mwAYk">Julia</a>: the <code id="mwAYo">Survival.jl</code> package includes the Kaplan–Meier estimator.<sup about="#mwt212" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-28" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-28"}}'><a href="#cite_note-28" id="mwAYs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAYw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAY0">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAY4">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
